- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** SHARED_QUALITY_ANCESTRY
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-018
  - EL-025
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the target distinguish signal from routine variation before changing the process?
  - Are measurements valid and linked to fitness for use rather than merely stable?
  - Would statistical machinery add value for this population or create false precision?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-017 — Explicit current baseline with controlled revision

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-017
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Explicit current baseline with controlled revision
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota standardized work (takt, sequence, standard WIP); TWI Job Instruction; later learning-system interpretation.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Specify the current work sequence, pace and standard inventory so it can be taught and managed.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Implicit practice makes deviation, training and improvement impossible to evaluate.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Uncontrolled variation, knowledge loss, untestable change and inconsistent training.
- **MECHANISM:** Version the best-known current method, scope, expected result and exceptions; train it; expose deviations; test and authorise revisions.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Work recurs, consistency matters, multiple people perform it or failure consequences require a known baseline.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For one-off low-risk work, retain a short decision record or checklist rather than a full standard-work sheet.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Worker input; valid current evidence; change and exception authority; review owner/date; ergonomic and safety assessment.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Repeatability, faster training, visible deviation and a platform for improvement.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Immutable procedure; stale paperwork; coercive pace; deskilling; suppressed expert judgement; standards written by distant staff; unauthorised workarounds.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Labour studies show formalisation can reduce autonomy and wellbeing; Toyota doctrine does not prove democratic control.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Reinterpreted from prescribed sequence toward an explicit, testable, provisional baseline with lifecycle controls.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** A scoped, versioned, teachable baseline with exceptions, evidence, local challenge rights, qualification, expiry and retirement.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A standard-work sheet is optional. Executable checks, runbooks, contracts or annotated examples can serve.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_HISTORICAL_MIXED_SOCIAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S004], [S008], [S009]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S028]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S020], [S031], [S041]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Historical evidence for explicit expiry/retirement inside Toyota is limited; those safeguards are evolved engineering additions.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-014
  - EL-022
  - EL-029
  - EL-040
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the system distinguish a current baseline from an immutable rule?
  - Who may deviate, propose revision, qualify the change and retire the standard?
  - Is the baseline still valid, usable and safe rather than paperwork compliance?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-018 — Direct observation combined with traceable system evidence

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-018
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Direct observation combined with traceable system evidence
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota genchi genbutsu; quality-control measurement; modern observability convergence.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Go to the actual place, see the actual work and grasp facts before deciding.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Reports and abstractions can hide real conditions, while local impressions can also misrepresent distributed systems.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Decision by hearsay, dashboard-only management, executive theatre and local anecdotal causation.
- **MECHANISM:** Observe work and users directly, then triangulate with traces, records, samples and affected-worker testimony; preserve provenance.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Material uncertainty exists about what actually happens or reports conflict with outcomes.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For well-instrumented, low-risk routine work, sample direct observation periodically and investigate exceptions.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Access; non-punitive observation; representative sampling; traceability; skill in distinguishing observation from inference.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Better problem framing, discovery of unrecorded work and stronger causal hypotheses.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Gemba walks as theatre; surveillance; observer effects; anecdotal overreach; privileging visible local work over invisible dependencies.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Direct seeing is neither sufficient nor neutral; distributed software, supply networks and latent harms require instrumentation and testimony.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Mature form treats direct observation as one evidence channel in a provenance-bearing triangulation.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Triangulate actual-work observation, system traces, outcome data and stakeholder accounts; label inference and uncertainty.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A scheduled gemba walk is not required; contextual inquiry, trace review or incident reconstruction may be better.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S010], [S068]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S029], [S060]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S020], [S031], [S061]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How much observation frequency is enough under rapid change, privacy constraints and distributed work?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** TEAM
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-016
  - EL-023
  - EL-025
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the system obtain evidence from actual work rather than reports alone?
  - Is direct observation triangulated with traces, outcomes and affected people's testimony?
  - Could the observation process distort work or become surveillance?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-019 — Evidence-bound problem solving and countermeasure qualification

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-019
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Evidence-bound problem solving and countermeasure qualification
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota kaizen/problem solving, SQC/TQC, Ohno's Five Whys; Shewhart/Deming PDSA ancestry.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Investigate an abnormality, ask why, try a countermeasure and standardise successful change.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Symptoms recur because fixes are selected without causal evidence or tested only once.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Template-driven action, single-root stories, recurrence and false learning.
- **MECHANISM:** Define problem and baseline; gather evidence; develop competing causal hypotheses; predict effects; test safely; compare results; check side effects and recurrence.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** The failure is material, recurrent, causally uncertain or costly enough to justify investigation.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a trivial one-off reversible issue, fix and record the observation; escalate only on recurrence.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Time and skill; data/provenance; ability to test; independent challenge; authority over causes; follow-up owner.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Fewer recurrences, stronger learning and countermeasures that survive outside the initial event.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** A3/5-Whys ritual; confirmation bias; singular root cause; weak controls; no prediction; post hoc success; political exclusion of structural causes.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Format-specific causal evidence is weak; reviews show PDSA often used without core features and RCA often produces weak actions.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Moved from named templates toward explicit scientific reasoning, systems causation and countermeasure qualification.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** A proportional causal-learning loop with competing hypotheses, predictions, safe tests, outcome/side-effect evidence and recurrence review.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A3 paper, exactly five whys, fishbone or PDCA labels are optional prompts and never evidence.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** STRONGLY_RETAINED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONVERGENT_WEAK_FORMAT_SPECIFIC
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S007], [S010], [S012]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S045], [S046], [S048]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S043], [S047]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which problem classes justify experimentation, formal causal analysis or independent review remains context-dependent.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** HYBRID
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-020
  - EL-024
  - EL-038
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does repeated failure produce competing causal hypotheses and a qualified countermeasure rather than a completed template?
  - Are predictions, disconfirming evidence, side effects and recurrence checked?
  - Would a cheap fix suffice, or is the causal uncertainty consequential enough to trigger the full loop?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-020 — Improvement sustainment, expiry, retirement and economics

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-020
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Improvement sustainment, expiry, retirement and economics
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Kaizen follow-up, standardisation after improvement; later sustainment and deimplementation research.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Standardise a successful improvement so gains persist.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Changes decay, create maintenance burden, outlive their premise or continue after value disappears.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Improvement theatre, regression, zombie controls and unbounded process complexity.
- **MECHANISM:** Assign owner, expected benefit, maintenance cost, verification interval, expiry/renewal rule, rollback and retirement evidence.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** A countermeasure changes recurring work or introduces continuing control/maintenance cost.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For ephemeral low-risk changes, define a short observation period and automatic expiry.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Baseline and outcome data; ownership; ability to remove controls; preserved rationale; leadership continuity.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Durable gains, lower bureaucracy, timely removal of obsolete controls and transparent improvement economics.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Audit-only sustainment; permanent controls after risk changes; sunk-cost defence; leadership turnover; benefits not remeasured.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Kaizen-event studies show human outcomes and gains require follow-up; local improvements often lack customer or system outcome evidence.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Added lifecycle, deimplementation and maintenance economics to 'standardise and spread'.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Every material improvement is a governed hypothesis with benefit, burden, review, expiry and retirement path.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Follow-up audits or control plans are implementations; evidence-bearing lifecycle ownership is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_EMPIRICAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S020]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S046], [S048], [S060]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S043], [S044], [S061]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Long-term comparative evidence on retirement and deimplementation inside Lean programmes is limited.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** LEAN_PRODUCTION_GENERALISATION
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-017
  - EL-038
  - EL-040
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Who owns the improvement after launch, and when is benefit rechecked?
  - Can obsolete standards, controls and metrics expire or be retired?
  - Does maintenance cost exceed the current value of the improvement?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-021 — Worker capability, training and cross-skilling

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-021
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Worker capability, training and cross-skilling
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota supervisor/operator development, TWI Job Instruction, standard work and quality-circle traditions.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Train workers and supervisors to perform, recognise abnormality and improve the work.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** A tightly coupled system fails when knowledge is tacit, narrow or unavailable at the point of need.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Skill bottlenecks, unsafe improvisation, slow recovery and dependence on a few experts.
- **MECHANISM:** Define required capabilities; train through demonstration and practice; verify competence; cross-skill for response; preserve coaching time.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Work recurs, failures require local response, or capacity flexibility depends on transferable skill.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For rare low-risk work, retain accessible guidance and an expert escalation path rather than broad certification.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Paid training time; qualified instructors; humane workload; current baselines; opportunities to practise; recognition of expertise.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Safer execution, flexible capacity, faster abnormality response and more credible local improvement.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Training as compliance; skill matrices without ability; unpaid improvement burden; multiskilling used only to cut staffing; obsolete instruction.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Participation and cross-skilling can intensify work and widen responsibility without control, pay or security.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From job instruction and narrow shop skills toward socio-technical capability, coaching, recovery practice and knowledge continuity.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Maintain verified capability and cross-coverage for material work, paired with authority, workload protection and fair recognition.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** TWI cards, dojos and skill matrices are optional; demonstrated capability and coverage are the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_HISTORICAL_MIXED_EMPIRICAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S006], [S008], [S009]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S028]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S031], [S041], [S070]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Evidence separating training benefit from selection, staffing and management quality is incomplete.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** TEAM
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-015
  - EL-023
  - EL-035
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Do people have trained capability to execute, detect abnormality, recover and improve—not just responsibility?
  - Is cross-skilling supported by time, pay and staffing rather than used as headcount extraction?
  - Is competence verified and refreshed when the baseline changes?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-022 — Safe voice, participation and bounded change authority

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-022
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Safe voice, participation and bounded change authority
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota respect-for-human doctrine, suggestion systems, quality circles, andon and NUMMI involvement.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Workers participate in operating and improving the workshop and can call attention to abnormalities.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** People closest to work possess information but cannot safely expose or change problems.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Suppressed alarms, learned helplessness, management blindness and pseudo-participation.
- **MECHANISM:** Provide non-punitive signal, response and appeal channels; specify local/team/cross-functional change rights; protect dissent and stop authority.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Front-line observation is necessary for quality, safety or improvement and management cannot centrally see conditions.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For simple low-risk work, a direct owner contact and documented response is enough.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Employment/psychological safety; response obligation; workload and time; representation; transparent decisions; escalation above local management.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Earlier problem exposure, richer evidence, faster local correction and greater legitimacy.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Suggestion quotas; ignored andons; consultation without decision rights; retaliation; responsibility without resources; surveillance.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Toyota and Lean rhetoric often labels involvement 'empowerment' while pace, staffing and strategic decisions remain managerial.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Mature form distinguishes voice, information, consent, local change authority, stop authority and bargaining power.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** A governed rights-and-response system: safe signal, bounded local action, appeal, documented disposition and protection against workload transfer.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Suggestion boxes, quality circles and daily huddles are optional; real voice and decision rights are the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MIXED_EMPIRICAL_HIGH_NORMATIVE
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S006], [S013], [S015]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S029]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S027], [S028], [S031]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which governance arrangements reliably preserve voice under cost and schedule pressure across labour regimes?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-007
  - EL-012
  - EL-017
  - EL-023
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Can workers safely signal, stop, challenge and propose change, and must the organisation respond?
  - What decisions are worker-local, team, supervisory, cross-functional or system-owner?
  - Does participation change decisions or merely supply unpaid improvement labour?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-023 — Employment security, trust and fair distribution of productivity gains

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-023
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Employment security, trust and fair distribution of productivity gains
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota's post-1950 labour compact and later joint declarations; NUMMI employment-security practice.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Link productivity improvement with stable employment, working conditions and labour-management trust.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Workers rationally conceal improvement opportunities when improvement threatens jobs, workload or bargaining position.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Trust destruction, sabotage, hidden problems, fear-based compliance and 'lean and mean' layoffs.
- **MECHANISM:** Set credible no-layoff or redeployment rules for improvement gains where feasible; negotiate workload; share gains; protect reporting and transition.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Improvement solicits worker knowledge, reduces labour content or changes job design materially.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For small changes with no employment/workload effect, state that boundary and monitor cumulative effects.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Credible management commitment; financial capacity; worker representation/voice; redeployment and training; transparent gain accounting.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** More truthful problem exposure, cooperation, capability retention and lower fear.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Promises withdrawn in downturns; gains captured by owners; redeployment becomes intensification; insiders protected while suppliers/contractors absorb risk.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Toyota's 1950 dispute and wider Lean layoffs show security is not inherent; national institutions and firm economics matter.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Moved from implicit Japanese/NUMMI compact to explicit socio-technical prerequisite and fair-transition requirement.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Before labour-saving improvement, define employment, workload, compensation, redeployment and supplier effects; treat trust as evidence-bearing governance.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Respect-for-people slogans, town halls and culture statements do not establish security or fair gains.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MIXED_CONTEXTUAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S013], [S014], [S015]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S029], [S070]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S026], [S027], [S031]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** No universal employment-security design exists for volatile firms, contractors or supply networks; this remains institution-dependent.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-006
  - EL-009
  - EL-015
  - EL-035
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Could surfacing or removing work threaten employment, pay, workload or supplier viability?
  - What credible protections and gain-sharing arrangements make participation rational?
  - Are labour harms measured alongside flow and cost outcomes?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-024 — Management responsibility for system conditions and escalation response

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-024
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Management responsibility for system conditions and escalation response
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota supervisory/management system, TQC and later TPS learning-system interpretations.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Managers create conditions, respond to abnormalities and support workers in solving problems.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Local teams are held accountable for causes created by staffing, incentives, architecture, contracts or policy.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Blame shifting, repeated local fixes, ignored escalation and transformation decay.
- **MECHANISM:** Assign accountable system owners; require timely response; remove cross-boundary impediments; fund capability/buffers; review whether incentives create the failure.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** A problem exceeds local authority, recurs across teams or reflects structural operating conditions.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a bounded local issue, delegate action with a clear escalation threshold.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Authority over resources and policy; transparent response expectations; independent review; outcome and labour measures.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Structural causes addressed, credible stop/voice systems and sustained improvement.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Leader gemba theatre; delegation without resources; hero intervention; changing priorities; local blame; executive metrics conflict with doctrine.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Proponent accounts often make management commitment an unfalsifiable explanation for success or failure.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Shifted from supervisor response to explicit system ownership, governance and accountability for incentives and preconditions.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Leaders own the conditions they control, respond to escalations, arbitrate cross-system trade-offs and are measured on outcomes, resilience and worker effects.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Leader standard work, gemba walks and coaching kata are optional; accountable system condition ownership is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** STRONGLY_RETAINED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005], [S010], [S013]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S024], [S029], [S030]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S027], [S030], [S044]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How can management responsibility be measured without turning 'leadership commitment' into a post hoc success explanation?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-022
  - EL-025
  - EL-038
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Are repeated local failures traceable to conditions only management or a system owner can change?
  - Does escalation produce a bounded response and decision?
  - Are leaders accountable for labour, resilience and incentive side effects, not only throughput?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-025 — Whole-system optimisation and proxy/metric governance

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-025
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Whole-system optimisation and proxy/metric governance
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** TPS anti-overproduction and system flow; Lean 'optimise the whole'; modern operations and software measurement.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Subordinate local production to the needs of the downstream process and whole value stream.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Teams game local throughput, cycle time, defect, utilisation or productivity metrics while system outcomes deteriorate.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Local optimisation, Goodhart effects, hidden queues, quality sacrifice and worker harm.
- **MECHANISM:** Use a balanced causal measurement set; define system boundary and countermetrics; avoid individual competition; audit gaming and revise definitions.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Metrics affect incentives or decisions across interacting components.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a small team with direct outcome visibility, use a few narrative/quantitative checks rather than a dashboard programme.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Outcome model; stable metric definitions; cross-boundary data; qualitative challenge; no punishment for adverse signals.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Better decisions, fewer proxy pathologies and alignment of flow, quality, resilience, value and wellbeing.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Dashboard proliferation; composite-score opacity; denominator manipulation; item splitting; suppressed incidents; cross-context ranking; measurement burden.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Lean measures are frequently used for labour control; DORA and SPACE explicitly warn against single metrics and inappropriate comparisons.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From local flow and waste measures to multidimensional system indicators, countermetrics and explicit anti-gaming governance.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Treat metrics as revisable evidence, not targets or truth; pair throughput with quality, outcome, resilience and human effects.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Obeya boards, SQDC panels, burndowns or DORA dashboards are optional; decision-valid measurement is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** USEFUL_BUT_EASILY_GAMED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005], [S018], [S053]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S065]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S058], [S059], [S062]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How can metric gaming be detected before it materially distorts the work?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** HYBRID
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-001
  - EL-003
  - EL-009
  - EL-023
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Do metrics represent the whole outcome or create a local target that can be gamed?
  - Are quality, resilience, wellbeing and hidden queues countermeasured?
  - Can definitions and incentives be revised when behaviour adapts to the metric?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-026 — Equipment, service and delivery-system reliability and maintainability

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-026
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Equipment, service and delivery-system reliability and maintainability
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** TPS equipment availability and preventive maintenance; TPM quality ancestry; modern reliability engineering.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Maintain equipment capability so low-WIP flow is not repeatedly interrupted.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Tight flow exposes breakdowns, but exposure without reliability capability creates service failure.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Unplanned downtime, repeated emergency repair, unsafe degradation and buffer depletion.
- **MECHANISM:** Identify critical assets/services; monitor failure and degradation; perform risk-based preventive/predictive maintenance; design for diagnosis, repair and recovery.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Failure interrupts flow materially, has safety/quality consequences or consumes strategic buffers.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For noncritical, cheap and rapidly replaceable components, use run-to-failure with spares and a tested replacement path.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Criticality model; maintenance windows and capacity; skilled responders; spares; configuration history; feedback from incidents.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Higher availability, lower variability, safer operation and smaller required buffers.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Maintenance theatre; over-maintenance; bad predictive models; deferred maintenance to hit throughput; hidden technical debt; single-expert dependence.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Low inventory can convert ordinary failures into crises, while TPM programmes can become checklist bundles without reliability economics.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From factory preventive maintenance to service reliability, observability, error budgets, graceful degradation and recovery engineering.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Choose maintenance and recovery policy by criticality, failure distribution, consequence and replacement economics.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** TPM pillars, maintenance boards and autonomous-maintenance checklists are optional implementations.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** STRONGLY_RETAINED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005], [S004]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S032], [S039]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S041], [S042], [S057]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How should maintenance policy incorporate correlated software, cyber and supplier failures with sparse history?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** SHARED_QUALITY_ANCESTRY
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Reliability reduces variability and buffer demand. Over-maintenance adds cost; under-maintenance causes disruption. Cheap path is tested run-to-failure for noncritical assets.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-006
  - EL-008
  - EL-009
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the target identify reliability-critical equipment, services and dependencies?
  - Is maintenance chosen from failure and recovery economics rather than ritual frequency?
  - Would reducing buffers expose an unaddressed reliability defect?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-027 — Supplier and dependency capability development with fair risk allocation

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-027
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Supplier and dependency capability development with fair risk allocation
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota supplier associations, quality/TPS training and interfirm learning network.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Develop suppliers' quality, delivery and improvement capability rather than inspect or bargain only on price.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** End-to-end performance is limited by external capability and incentives outside the focal organisation.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Recurring supplier defects, unstable replenishment, adversarial cost transfer and weak joint learning.
- **MECHANISM:** Share requirements and knowledge; jointly solve capability gaps; use fair contracts, gain/risk sharing and escalation; measure supplier health as well as price.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** A dependency is recurrent, strategically material and improvable through relationship-specific learning.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For commodity, low-risk purchases, use clear specifications, diversified sources and routine qualification rather than a development programme.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Long-term relationship; technical openness; bargaining fairness; supplier capacity; protection of proprietary knowledge; exit alternatives.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Higher quality, faster learning, less disruption and lower total cost.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Coercive price reduction; unpaid improvement; lock-in; focal-firm appropriation of gains; supplier overinvestment; lower-tier labour harm.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Toyota partnership narratives can obscure power asymmetry; synchronisation may transfer buffers, capital and risk to suppliers.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Mature form adds supplier viability, fair gain/risk allocation, labour standards, lower-tier visibility and recovery planning.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Develop critical dependency capability only where joint value exceeds coordination and lock-in cost, with explicit fairness and resilience safeguards.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Supplier kaizen workshops, scorecards or associations are optional; joint capability and fair allocation are the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** SOCIO_TECHNICAL_CONTEXT_PROPERTY
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_EMPIRICAL_MIXED_DISTRIBUTIONAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005], [S010], [S069]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S039], [S040]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S038], [S041]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Independent evidence on how Toyota-style supplier development distributes gains, workload and risk across tiers remains incomplete.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** DIRECT_TPS_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-008
  - EL-023
  - EL-028
  - EL-039
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Is a critical supplier being developed or merely pressured to absorb cost and inventory?
  - Are gains, buffers, workload and disruption risk allocated transparently and fairly?
  - Would diversification be cheaper and safer than deep relationship-specific development?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-028 — Multi-tier dependency visibility and resilience segmentation

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-028
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Multi-tier dependency visibility and resilience segmentation
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Post-2011 Toyota supply-chain mapping and broader resilience research.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** After disruption, identify lower-tier sites, affected items, inventories and restoration alternatives.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Tier-one visibility hides common sub-tier dependencies, concentration and long recovery paths.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Correlated disruption, unknown single points of failure, slow recovery and false diversification.
- **MECHANISM:** Map critical item/site dependencies beyond the first tier; classify by lead time, substitutability and consequence; prepare restoration, alternate design/source and data-maintenance routines.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** A dependency can stop a material outcome, has long recovery, opaque tiers or geographic/technical concentration.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For low-criticality, rapidly substitutable inputs, record vendor/category and test replacement rather than map the whole network.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Supplier cooperation; data governance; item-site identity; criticality model; alternative qualification; regular updates.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Earlier disruption recognition, targeted buffers and faster alternate sourcing or redesign.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Huge stale maps; supplier confidentiality conflict; false sense of control; concentration persists despite visibility; mapping cost exceeds value.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Mapping does not itself create resilience and can shift reporting burden to small suppliers.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From JIT synchronisation to segmented JIT/JIC, virtual dualisation, critical-stock and recoverability design.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Maintain decision-useful deep-tier visibility only for critical dependencies, coupled to buffer, alternate and recovery action.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A proprietary supply-chain map or Toyota RESCUE system is not required; a critical-dependency register may suffice.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** STRONGLY_RETAINED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_POST_DISRUPTION_DOMAIN
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S035], [S036]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S039], [S040]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S037], [S040]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Methods for validating supplier-reported lower-tier data and common-cause dependence remain immature.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** REACTION_TO_LEAN
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Resilience profile: visibility does not reduce flow by itself; it enables selective buffers, alternates and recovery. Overmapping wastes effort; undermapping hides correlated failure.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-027
  - EL-034
  - EL-039
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the system know which lower-tier dependencies can stop the outcome and how long recovery takes?
  - Is visibility linked to an alternate, buffer, redesign or restoration decision?
  - Can low-criticality dependencies follow a cheap path instead of exhaustive mapping?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-029 — Set-based option preservation and delayed irreversible convergence

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-029
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Set-based option preservation and delayed irreversible convergence
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toyota product-development research and set-based concurrent engineering.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Explore ranges of alternatives, communicate constraints and narrow sets as feasibility evidence accumulates.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Early point decisions create lock-in, late redesign and fragile optimisation under uncertainty.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Premature convergence, expensive reversal and loss of feasible alternatives.
- **MECHANISM:** Map design space and constraints; keep multiple feasible options; run discriminating tests; eliminate options with evidence; commit at the last responsible point.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Uncertainty, coupling and irreversibility are high enough that a wrong early choice is costly and parallel learning is affordable.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** When the decision is reversible, familiar or low-coupling, choose one option and preserve a fallback note.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Capacity for parallel analysis; explicit decision criteria; integration/trade-off knowledge; convergence deadlines; ownership.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Reduced late rework, better trade-offs and more robust commitment.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Unbounded option proliferation; delayed decisions; duplicated work; superficial sets; hidden resource cost; no convergence rule.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Industrial implementation maturity and causal performance evidence remain limited; not every domain benefits.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Separated from manufacturing waste elimination and reframed as an uncertainty/option-value mechanism.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Preserve only decision-relevant alternatives until evidence changes expected regret; record eliminated options and convergence rationale.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Trade-off curves, set matrices and obeya rooms are optional; evidence-calibrated option preservation is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_CASE_BASED
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S049], [S050]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S051], [S052]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S049], [S052]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Strong comparative causal evidence outside automotive/product design remains limited.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** LEAN_PRODUCT_DEVELOPMENT_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Exploration intentionally creates parallel WIP; favour it where option value exceeds queue and coordination cost. Conflicts with premature flow optimisation.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-004
  - EL-005
  - EL-007
  - EL-036
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Is uncertainty and irreversibility high enough to justify preserving alternatives?
  - What evidence eliminates an option, and when must convergence occur?
  - Would parallel exploration reduce expected rework or merely duplicate effort?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-030 — Cross-functional product-development integration and reusable trade-off knowledge

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-030
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Cross-functional product-development integration and reusable trade-off knowledge
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Comparative automotive product-development research and Toyota LPD interpretations.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Integrate product, process and organisational knowledge under strong project leadership; reuse constraints and trade-offs.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Functional silos discover incompatibility late and repeatedly relearn design constraints.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Late engineering changes, handoff loss, manufacturing/service incompatibility and repeated analysis.
- **MECHANISM:** Create cross-functional decision forums; maintain constraint interfaces, trade-off evidence and reusable knowledge; give project leadership authority to resolve conflicts.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Product outcomes couple multiple disciplines, lifecycle stages or downstream operations.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a small single-discipline change, direct peer review and a concise decision record suffice.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Representative disciplines; decision authority; shared models; knowledge stewardship; incentives for lifecycle rather than functional optimisation.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Faster convergence, fewer late changes and compounding design knowledge.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Meeting overload; heavyweight bureaucracy; knowledge repositories without use; dominant function suppresses dissent; outdated trade-off curves.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Automotive evidence may not transfer; LPD lacks agreed definition and complete coherent implementation evidence.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From heavyweight project management toward digital thread, model-based trade-offs, concurrent assurance and learning reuse.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Integrate affected disciplines at consequential decisions and preserve reusable evidence at interfaces—not every participant in every meeting.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Obeya rooms, chief-engineer titles and trade-off curves are optional implementations.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_COMPARATIVE_AUTOMOTIVE
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S049], [S050]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S051], [S052]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S049], [S052]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Which integration structures work best outside automotive and how reusable knowledge remains valid under technology change are unresolved.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** LEAN_PRODUCT_DEVELOPMENT_LINEAGE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-002
  - EL-020
  - EL-029
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Are coupled disciplines represented before irreversible decisions?
  - Is trade-off and constraint evidence reusable and freshness-controlled?
  - Would an integration mechanism resolve actual late conflict or only create more meetings?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-031 — Software delivery flow with automation, observability, reliability and recovery

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-031
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Software delivery flow with automation, observability, reliability and recovery
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Lean software/Kanban translations combined with Continuous Delivery, DevOps and reliability engineering.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Translate flow, small batches, quality-at-source and WIP limitation into software work.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Software changes wait in queues, integrate late, fail opaquely or reach production without safe recovery.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Large risky releases, slow feedback, hidden operational defects and speed-at-the-expense-of-stability.
- **MECHANISM:** Limit active work; integrate frequently; automate build/test/deploy; observe production; use progressive delivery, rollback and incident learning; validate user outcomes.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Software changes are frequent enough that manual release and delayed integration impose risk or delay.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For infrequent low-risk software, use reproducible builds, basic automated tests and a documented release/recovery path.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Version control; trustworthy tests; environment/configuration control; observability; on-call/recovery capability; product outcome feedback.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Faster and safer change, shorter learning cycles and reduced release blast radius.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Pipeline theatre; flaky tests; metric gaming; developer burnout; rapid delivery of low-value work; fragile platforms; operations burden hidden.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Software evidence is largely observational and case-based; factory metaphors miss discovery, architecture and invisible cognitive work.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Kanban's flow ideas were hybridised with software-native automation, reliability, security and observability.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Optimise sustainable software change across throughput and instability, with small reversible changes, outcome validation and humane operations.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A kanban board, daily deployment or DORA dashboard is not required; reliable, observable and reversible delivery capability is the property.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_LARGE_OBSERVATIONAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S053], [S054], [S057]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S055], [S056], [S059]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S055], [S056], [S058]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Causal effect sizes, optimal limits and metric robustness across software domains remain unresolved.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** HYBRID
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CROSS_FUNCTIONAL
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Value: shorter software feedback and smaller failure scope. Assumes automation quality, operability and recovery. Overuse creates metric gaming, toil and acceleration without value.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-001
  - EL-004
  - EL-023
  - EL-025
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Can the system deliver small changes safely, observe effects and recover without heroics?
  - Are delivery metrics used as system signals rather than individual targets?
  - Does faster delivery improve user/system outcomes or merely increase change volume?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-032 — Portfolio selection, stopping and retirement of low-value work

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-032
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Portfolio selection, stopping and retirement of low-value work
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Lean value/waste generalisation, product-development learning and modern product/flow management.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Eliminate work that does not create value and stop overproduction.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Organisations optimise execution while accepting too much low-value work and retaining obsolete products, controls or commitments.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** Portfolio congestion, zombie work, sunk-cost continuation and capacity starvation.
- **MECHANISM:** Set admission, continuation and retirement criteria; compare expected value and learning against total cost; stop, defer or archive work explicitly.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** Demand exceeds capacity, work ages without evidence, or a value hypothesis fails.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** For a tiny backlog, review informally at a defined interval and close stale items.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Outcome evidence; total-cost visibility; authority to stop; reversible archival/transition; stakeholder communication.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Freed capacity, lower WIP, less maintenance burden and focus on consequential outcomes.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Politically convenient cancellation; short-termism; abandoned users; hidden transition costs; gaming by redefining work as mandatory.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Waste language can erase care, resilience or exploratory work whose value is indirect or delayed.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** From eliminating production waste to governing portfolio entry, continuation, deimplementation and retirement.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Stop or retire work only through an evidence-bearing decision that accounts for beneficiary, option value, transition and external costs.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Backlog purges, stop-start-continue workshops and portfolio kanban are optional.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** MODERATE_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S018], [S053]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S060], [S065]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S044], [S058], [S062]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** How should long-horizon public value and option value be preserved when near-term evidence is weak?

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** CONVERGENT_ENGINEERING
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-001
  - EL-008
  - EL-029
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Can low-value or stale work be stopped, deferred and retired rather than merely completed faster?
  - Does the stop decision account for transition, users, resilience and option value?
  - Is a lightweight periodic review sufficient for this portfolio?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** —

### EL-033 — Universal waste elimination as the master objective

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-033
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Universal waste elimination as the master objective
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Later Lean simplification of Ohno's waste categories and Lean Thinking.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Identify and eliminate all non-value-adding activity.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Real waste can consume capacity, obscure defects and delay value.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None as a universal master objective; the maxim itself can create harm.
- **MECHANISM:** As historically deployed, classify activity as waste and remove it.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Specific waste removal must be justified through EL-001, EL-002, EL-008 and outcome evidence.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Name the concrete failure mode and remove only the activity that causes it; do not launch generic waste hunts.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Valid value model, full system boundary, labour/resilience analysis and evidence that the activity is avoidable.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Can remove genuine delay, rework or unnecessary effort when narrowly applied.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Removes resilience, learning, care, maintenance, worker discretion or necessary redundancy; becomes headcount reduction.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Waste is easier to name than value; universal elimination ignores variability, option value and externalities.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Superseded by explicit outcome, queue, buffer, reliability and portfolio mechanisms.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** No independent property. Ask which failure mode the alleged waste causes and what protective function it may serve.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Waste walks, seven/eight-waste posters and red-tag events are not evidence.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CRITICAL_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S007], [S018]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S024], [S025], [S065]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S032], [S033], [S060]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** The boundary between avoidable burden and protective slack remains context-specific, not a universal taxonomy.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-001
  - EL-008
  - EL-023
  - EL-029
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - What concrete failure mode does the alleged waste cause?
  - Does the activity provide resilience, learning, safety, care or option value?
  - Would removing it improve the whole outcome or only a local cost metric?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected as an independent crosswalk property; retained only as a prompt subordinate to stronger properties.

### EL-034 — Minimum or zero inventory everywhere

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-034
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Minimum or zero inventory everywhere
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Toolkit caricature of JIT and low-stock Lean programmes.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Drive inventory toward zero to expose problems and release capital.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Excess stock can hide instability, delay feedback and consume capital.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None at universal scope; the maxim creates unbuffered risk.
- **MECHANISM:** Remove inventory and rely on replenishment or expediting.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Inventory reduction is justified only after component-specific buffer economics.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Measure criticality, lead time, obsolescence and recovery; remove only demonstrably excess stock.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Reliable replenishment; alternate capacity; bounded demand/lead-time variability; recovery capability.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Lower carrying cost and faster defect feedback within a safe operating region.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Stockouts, production/service interruption, supplier pressure, emergency freight and correlated disruption.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Toyota sources specify minimum necessary stock, not zero; empirical performance is often concave and disruption research rejects binary JIT/JIC.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Replaced by EL-008 and EL-028: segmented buffer portfolios and dependency resilience.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** NO GENERAL PROPERTY; choose inventory by total expected cost and resilience.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Kanban quantity cuts and inventory-reduction targets are not inherently beneficial.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONTRARY_EVIDENCE
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S002], [S006], [S067]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S033], [S039]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S032], [S035], [S040]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Optimal stock under deep uncertainty and correlated shocks remains difficult, but that uncertainty strengthens rather than weakens the rejection of zero inventory.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Over-removal shifts variability to capacity/time and creates starvation. Mature substitute: EL-008.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-004
  - EL-008
  - EL-028
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Is inventory demonstrably excess after accounting for lead time, consequence and recovery?
  - Which buffer will absorb variability after inventory is removed?
  - Could the same benefit come from reliability or setup improvement without reducing protection?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected universal rule; component-specific inventory optimisation remains valid.

### EL-035 — Maximum utilisation and zero slack

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-035
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Maximum utilisation and zero slack
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Mass-production efficiency logic and cost-cutting Lean appropriations, not a defensible TPS invariant.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Keep labour and equipment continuously busy and remove idle capacity.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Visible idle resources can be costly, but under variability utilisation is not throughput.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None at universal scope.
- **MECHANISM:** Increase load, cut staffing and fill all available capacity.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. High planned utilisation may be acceptable only with low variability and cheap delay.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Measure queues and service risk; release spare capacity only where it has no response or resilience function.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Near-deterministic arrivals/service, low failure consequence and external buffers.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** May lower unit cost in a narrow stable operating region.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Nonlinear queues, burnout, no improvement/response time, brittle recovery and hidden overtime.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Operations science shows capacity is one of the required variability buffers; labour evidence links intensification to harm.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Rejected and replaced by queue-aware capacity plus explicit strategic slack.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Target sustainable service and total cost, not maximum busy time.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Utilisation dashboards, staffing-to-takt formulas and efficiency targets do not establish value.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONTRARY_EVIDENCE
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S070]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S032], [S027], [S028]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** The socially acceptable and economically optimal reserve varies by service obligation and risk.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** High utilisation under variability creates queues; substitute EL-008/EL-009.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-008
  - EL-009
  - EL-023
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Is spare capacity an unneeded cost or the system's response, learning and recovery buffer?
  - What queue and wellbeing effects follow the utilisation target?
  - Could demand shaping or reliability improvement close the cost gap instead?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected universal rule.

### EL-036 — One-piece flow or the smallest possible batch everywhere

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-036
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** One-piece flow or the smallest possible batch everywhere
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Factory ideal generalised beyond its economic conditions.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Move one item continuously through adjacent processes.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Large batches can create delay and rework.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None as a universal minimum-batch rule.
- **MECHANISM:** Force work into single-item or arbitrarily tiny units.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Use only where setup/coordination is low, routing is repeatable and one-unit movement improves economics.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Choose the smallest economically coherent and safely verifiable batch, not literal one-piece flow.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Low setup and transport cost; balanced reliable process; modular output; compatible validation.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Excellent feedback and low WIP in suitable repetitive flows.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Fragmentation, excessive handoffs, unsafe partial output, validation overhead, loss of system coherence and starvation.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Knowledge/product development may benefit from coherent experiments or parallel option sets; HMLV settings require adaptation.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Narrowed into EL-005 batch economics and EL-029 option-value reasoning.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** NO GENERAL PROPERTY; choose reversible economic batches by context.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** U-shaped cells, single-card movement and one-story-at-a-time rules are optional local designs.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_MECHANISTIC_CONTEXTUAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S004], [S007]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S032], [S052]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S057], [S063], [S064]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Batch economics under complex integration and shared validation remain an engineering-analysis problem.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** TEAM
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Small batches shorten feedback until setup/coordination/validation dominates. Substitute EL-005.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-005
  - EL-029
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - What is the economic and epistemic batch, including setup and validation?
  - Would a smaller unit remain coherent and safe?
  - Does literal one-piece flow solve a measured queue or import a factory ideal?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected universal rule; local one-piece flow can still be optimal.

### EL-037 — Exact Toyota-tool or named-method conformance

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-037
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Exact Toyota-tool or named-method conformance
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Global Lean diffusion through visible tools, consulting programmes and certification.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Install kanban, 5S, A3, VSM, standard-work sheets, andon and kaizen events as a Lean system.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Tools can embody useful mechanisms, but conformance is often mistaken for capability.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None as an independent property.
- **MECHANISM:** Audit presence, format and usage of named practices.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. A named practice should be chosen only if it is the cheapest effective implementation of a retained property.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Implement the property in the target system's native representation; document why no Lean artefact is needed.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Mechanism match, user fit, maintenance, decision impact and evidence.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Can accelerate communication where users share the practice and the form fits the work.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Cargo cult, duplicate tooling, audit theatre, resistance, stale artefacts and false maturity.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Empirical implementation research finds soft/system conditions distinguish outcomes more than possession of hard tools.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Rejected as methodology conformance; ceremony stripping maps every practice to mechanism and alternatives.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** NO GENERAL PROPERTY. Evaluate mechanism, trigger, burden, alternatives and outcome.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** This candidate is ceremony itself.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** CEREMONY_NOT_GENERAL_PROPERTY
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CONVERGENT
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S018]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S022], [S024], [S030]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S030], [S055], [S066]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Some forms may offer standardisation or communication benefits, but those must be evaluated per context.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-003
  - EL-017
  - EL-019
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Which retained property would this named practice implement?
  - Is the practice cheaper and more effective than a native alternative?
  - Would adoption close a failure mode or merely improve Lean conformance?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected as a property; all named practices are optional implementations.

### EL-038 — Improvement activity volume as evidence of improvement

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-038
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Improvement activity volume as evidence of improvement
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Suggestion counts, kaizen-event programmes, A3 completion and transformation dashboards.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Increase the number of suggestions, events, A3s, cycles or completed improvements.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Organisations need participation and follow-through, but activity is easier to count than effect.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None; the proxy creates a new failure mode.
- **MECHANISM:** Reward counts, completion and cadence.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Activity may be monitored diagnostically but not treated as outcome.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Track only material countermeasures and sample their benefit, burden and recurrence.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Outcome evidence, anti-gaming checks and no quota incentives.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** May reveal participation or process blockage as a secondary signal.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Trivial changes, paperwork, gaming, fatigue, hidden failures and permanent bureaucracy.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Kaizen/PDSA studies show method fidelity and sustainment vary; completed events do not establish customer/system payoff.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Rejected and replaced by EL-019, EL-020 and EL-025.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Count evidence-qualified outcomes and retired failures, not ritual volume.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** A3 counts, kaizen newspapers, suggestion quotas and event calendars are not engineering properties.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_CRITICAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S011]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S045], [S047], [S048]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S043], [S044], [S060]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Useful leading indicators of improvement capability remain context-specific and gameable.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** MANAGEMENT_ADOPTION
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-019
  - EL-020
  - EL-025
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does the improvement metric measure changed outcomes or only completed activity?
  - Can teams decline low-value events and retire ineffective controls without penalty?
  - How is gaming, fatigue and maintenance burden detected?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected as outcome evidence.

### EL-039 — Universal supplier synchronisation, concentration or single sourcing

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-039
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Universal supplier synchronisation, concentration or single sourcing
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Overgeneralisation of Toyota supplier/JIT coordination.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Tightly synchronise suppliers and reduce duplicated stocks/sources.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Uncoordinated supply can create inventory and quality problems.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None at universal scope; synchronisation can magnify common-cause failure.
- **MECHANISM:** Concentrate volume, shorten schedules and reduce supplier/stock redundancy.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Deep synchronisation is justified only where capability gains exceed concentration and disruption risk.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Use clear interfaces, qualified alternates and ordinary supplier management for nonstrategic inputs.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** Supplier viability; visibility; fair contracts; recovery; substitutability; geographic/technical risk analysis.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** May improve quality learning and transaction efficiency in stable, high-trust relationships.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Lock-in, supplier fragility, lower-tier single points, bargaining abuse and correlated disruption.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Post-2011 evidence and JIT risk studies show synchronisation/centralisation can raise disruption exposure.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Replaced by EL-027 joint capability plus EL-028 segmented resilience and alternatives.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** Choose relationship depth and source redundancy by criticality, learning value and recovery economics.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Milk runs, supplier kanban and single-source scorecards are not general requirements.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_POST_DISRUPTION
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S005], [S069]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S039], [S040]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S035], [S037], [S038]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** Optimal balance between relationship-specific learning and diversification remains industry-specific.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** TOOLKIT_CARICATURE
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** Tight synchronisation can lower routine buffers but raise disruption correlation. Substitute EL-027/EL-028.
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-027
  - EL-028
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Does deeper synchronisation improve joint capability enough to justify concentration risk?
  - Are alternate sources or designs truly independent and qualified?
  - Who carries inventory, capital and recovery burden across the tiers?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected universal rule; selective integration remains valid.

### EL-040 — Immutable standardisation and procedure compliance

- **PROPERTY_ID:** EL-040
- **PROPERTY_NAME:** Immutable standardisation and procedure compliance
- **HISTORICAL_ORIGIN:** Taylorist/managerial appropriation of standard work and audit-based Lean deployments.
- **ORIGINAL_FORM:** Define one best way and require conformance.
- **PROBLEM_IT_ADDRESSED:** Variation can be harmful, but frozen procedure prevents adaptation and learning.
- **FAILURE_MODE_IT_TRIES_TO_PREVENT:** None as an immutable rule.
- **MECHANISM:** Central authors prescribe and audit compliance; deviations are treated as worker failure.
- **TRIGGER_OR_CONTEXT:** NO GENERAL TRIGGER. Safety-critical immutable constraints may exist, but even they require controlled review.
- **NON_TRIGGER_OR_CHEAP_PATH:** Use EL-017: a current baseline with explicit exceptions, change rights and review.
- **DEPENDENCIES_OR_PRECONDITIONS:** If strict compliance is temporarily necessary: validated hazard case, scope, independent review, override/escalation and expiry.
- **EXPECTED_ENGINEERING_PAYOFF:** Can reduce unsafe variation for stable, validated critical steps.
- **KNOWN_FAILURE_MODES:** Stale procedure, hidden workarounds, deskilling, loss of expertise, coercion and inability to respond to novel conditions.
- **IMPORTANT_CRITICISMS:** Worker studies link workflow formalisation and autonomy loss to poorer outcomes; mature Lean interpretations describe standards as improvable baselines.
- **HOW_THE_PROPERTY_EVOLVED:** Rejected and replaced by lifecycle-controlled baseline plus human judgement and change authority.
- **MATURE_OR_EVOLVED_FORM:** No immutable general standard; use risk-calibrated constraints within a versioned and reviewable baseline.
- **CEREMONY_VS_PROPERTY:** Standard-work sheets, SOP audits and conformance scores are not proof of capability.
- **CURRENT_STATUS:** REJECTED_OR_DISFAVOURED
- **EVIDENCE_STRENGTH:** HIGH_MIXED_SOCIAL
- **PRIMARY_SOURCES:** [S004], [S008]
- **CRITICAL_SOURCES:** [S025], [S026], [S028]
- **EMPIRICAL_OR_DOMAIN_EVIDENCE:** [S020], [S031]
- **OPEN_QUESTIONS:** The boundary between mandatory safety constraints and expert discretion must be justified per hazard.

**Additional adjudication fields**

- **LINEAGE_CLASSIFICATION:** MANAGEMENT_ADOPTION
- **IMPROVEMENT_AUTHORITY:** SYSTEM_OWNER
- **FLOW_RESILIENCE_PROFILE:** NOT_PRIMARY_FLOW_PROPERTY
- **POSSIBLE_CONFLICTING_PROPERTY:**
  - EL-014
  - EL-017
  - EL-022
- **QUESTIONS_FOR_REPOSITORY_AUDIT:**
  - Is the rule a current evidence-backed baseline or treated as permanently correct?
  - Can experts raise exceptions and revise the rule through a controlled path?
  - Does compliance suppress evidence of a changing context or unsafe workload?
- **DISPOSITION_NOTE:** Rejected universal rule; risk-based mandatory constraints remain possible within EL-017.

## EVOLVED_LEAN_CEREMONY_STRIPPING_LEDGER

| PRACTICE | UNDERLYING_PROPERTY | WHAT_THE_PRACTICE_BUYS | WHEN_IT_FAILS | WHETHER_THE_PRACTICE_IS_REQUIRED | ALTERNATIVE_IMPLEMENTATIONS | MODERN_DISPOSITION | SOURCES |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Andon cord/button/light | EL-011 abnormality detection/signalling plus EL-012 bounded escalation/containment. | A low-friction, location-specific call for help and, where warranted, a pre-defined stop path. | Workers fear blame; support cannot arrive; alarms are noisy; throughput targets override response; every signal becomes a full stop. | NO | Automated alert, software circuit breaker, incident page, quality gate, escalation queue, spoken call with logged ownership. | Retain action-linked signalling and response tiers; do not copy a cord or assume signal equals stop. | [S001], [S004], [S005], [S067], [S031] |
| Kanban card/bin | EL-004 explicit WIP/release constraint plus EL-010 replenishment information integrity. | A simple decentralised authorisation and visible replenishment signal tied to a finite container/card population. | Cards do not match capacity or demand; hidden expediting bypasses limits; long lead times starve work; cards circulate despite stale information. | NO | CONWIP, electronic token, admission-control rule, queue capacity, service quota, replenishment threshold. | Retain explicit release/WIP logic; the physical card is one historical implementation. | [S003], [S005], [S006], [S032], [S054] |
| Heijunka box / levelling board | EL-007 demand-capacity pacing and levelling. | A concrete schedule for smoothing a repetitive mix and making deviations visible. | Demand, processing time or mix is too volatile; levelling pushes variability into backlog, overtime or suppliers; adherence becomes more important than service. | NO | Rolling capacity plan, workload control, dynamic scheduling, service-class reservation, probabilistic staffing. | Use only where aggregation and repetitive pacing are economically valid. | [S004], [S005], [S034], [S063], [S064] |
| Takt-time display | EL-007 explicit demand-capacity pacing and EL-003 state legibility. | A shared pace reference and early indication that a repetitive process cannot meet planned demand. | Work is non-repetitive or highly variable; averages conceal tail risk; workers are paced coercively; demand estimate is false. | NO | Capacity envelope, service-level budget, arrival/throughput control chart, deadline-risk forecast. | Manufacturing-specific in its classic form; do not translate every knowledge task into a takt clock. | [S004], [S006], [S063], [S064], [S026] |
| One-piece flow / manufacturing cell | EL-005 batch economics, EL-006 setup reduction and EL-002 end-to-end flow. | Short feedback, low transfer batch and fewer queues when routing and setup economics permit. | Switching/validation cost is high; process times are imbalanced; shared specialist equipment is scarce; continuity risk rises. | NO | Small transfer batches, continuous integration, decoupling point, mixed flow with explicit buffers. | Treat batch size as an optimisation variable; reject smallest-possible-batch dogma. | [S004], [S032], [S033] |
| Value-stream map | EL-002 end-to-end queue/handoff visibility and EL-025 whole-system optimisation. | A shared snapshot of touch time, waiting, information and handoffs across a selected path. | The map is stale, local, ceremonial or linearises exploratory work; no owner can change cross-boundary causes. | NO | Event-log analysis, trace sample, dependency graph, service blueprint, process mining, causal pathway model. | Use the cheapest current model that changes a decision; wall-sized notation has no intrinsic value. | [S018], [S022], [S060], [S061] |
| 5S programme/audit | EL-003 workplace/work-state legibility and selected safety/search controls. | Common locations, reduced search and easier recognition of missing or abnormal physical state. | Tidiness is scored instead of outcomes; surveillance and unpaid upkeep grow; digital/knowledge work gains little; improvements decay. | NO | Naming conventions, ergonomic layout, inventory location control, state schema, automated stale-state checks. | Retain legibility where search/ambiguity is costly; reject ritual scores and universal 5S campaigns. | [S066], [S025], [S028] |
| Shadow board / floor marking | EL-003 point-of-work state legibility. | Immediate recognition of missing tools, expected location or work boundary. | The visual cue is irrelevant, cluttered, obsolete or used as compliance theatre. | NO | Tool tracking, labelled storage, digital inventory, contextual checklist. | Useful only when absence/location causes a material failure mode. | [S004], [S066] |
| Standard-work sheet | EL-017 explicit current baseline with controlled revision. | Teachable sequence, expected conditions and observable point of comparison. | The sheet is stale, authored without operators, treated as immutable or detached from actual conditions and exceptions. | NO | Versioned runbook, executable test, checklist, reference implementation, decision table, annotated demonstration. | Require baseline identity, scope, exceptions, owner, revision authority and review/retirement—not a Toyota form. | [S004], [S008], [S020], [S026], [S031] |
| A3 report | EL-019 evidence-bound problem framing, causal inquiry, countermeasure and qualification. | A compact shared reasoning record and coaching object. | One-page constraint compresses uncertainty; retrospective story replaces prediction; template completion becomes approval theatre. | NO | Experiment memo, incident review, causal graph, hypothesis log, design decision record, statistical analysis. | Retain concise traceable reasoning only where it improves a decision; exact paper size/boxes are ceremony. | [S020], [S045], [S046], [S047], [S048] |
